One `#` heading per family, one `##` heading per subfamily (A to Z) with one table, one row per significant feature change, bug fix or rebalance, sorted by
status (the order below), then by row number. **Complete** rows are not kept in the tables: they move to the [archive](#archive) at the bottom (done 2026-10-26). Detail stays in the linked writeups
([feature_writeup/README.md](../README.md) is the feature index); a row says enough to pick the work up and points at
the rest.

## How to read a row

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| # | Row number inside its subfamily; a row's id is `family/sub #n` from the `#` and `##` headings it sits under, e.g. `cans/canning #3`. Numbers stay with the row when the table is re-sorted; new rows take the next free number; a row that moves family gets a new number there |
| Change | The feature, fix or rebalance, in one line |
| Status | See below |
| Work | Where the work lands: **patcherMod** (the in-tree Forge mod), **new jar** (a new or other in-tree mod: the ports, More Mooblooms, Incubation Additions), **KubeJS**, **data** (json / datapack / generated), **config**, **tool** (`tools/*.py`), **mods/** (add or remove a jar), **docs** |
| Details | How it is (or would be) built, and what is left |
| Decisions | What has to be decided before or during the work; "none" when it can just be done |
| Writeup | The detailed writeup and section, plus its pending in-game tests |

Status, in table order within a subfamily (Bug first):

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Bug** | A known bug that is not fixed yet |
| **Planned** | Not started, no progress |
| **Pinned** | Planned, held on purpose until something else happens (the row says what) |
| **In Progress** | Started, not complete |
| **Testing** | Implementation complete, real performance unknown: not built into the pack yet, built but not run in game, run only on a scratch server or in a GameTest, or live too briefly (1.0.6 and later) to count as played |
| **Complete** | Tested enough from real play to count as functional; its writeup's test cases may still be open. Set for features live since 1.0.4 or earlier that are part of everyday play with nothing in their writeup saying they were never seen in game |

When a row is done: tick its tests in the writeup, set **Complete**, and at the next sweep move it to the [archive](#archive) (ids never change, so citations keep working; never renumber). New ideas go in the [scratchpad](scratchpad.md) first and
move here when they get a family.

## Families

Cooking families first, pack and tooling last.

- [food](#food): drinks, drugs, placeable food, seasoning, stack sizes, types and tuning
- [cans](#cans): canning (types, textures, crafts)
- [cooking](#cooking): kitchen blocks and tools, remainder buffer, oven
- [baking](#baking): baked goods, sweets
- [dishes](#dishes): ingredients, jams, meals, ocean foods, pickles and ferments
- [farming](#farming): crops, wild crops, composting, trees
- [worldgen](#worldgen): locate, biomes, nether blocks in biomes, flowers, bee nests, seasons
- [structures](#structures): rates and groups, metas, earth fill, terrain, contents, net-new, nether block swap, testing
- [mobs](#mobs): spawns, pillagers, mooblooms, nests, tweaks and bugs, bees
- [loot](#loot): tables, LootJS layer, Lootr, wandering trader
- [gear](#gear): tuning, echo tier, shields, archery, camera bags
- [potions](#potions): potions, potion buckets, effects (mechanics), enchanting
- [recipes](#recipes): progression, engineering, metal, wood, cooking, magic, materials
- [engineering](#engineering): sawmill and components, Dreams and Desires
- [gameplay](#gameplay): time, blockrunner, TNT, portal, misc
- [items](#items): item physics, jars, storage and item loss, starter kit
- [blocks](#blocks): new and backported blocks, framed / copycat, decor, dyeing
- [ui](#ui): changelog, tooltips, names, effects text, WTHIT, toasts, misc
- [jei](#jei): JEI coverage, JEI ordering
- [alcohol](#alcohol): Brewin' port, Vinery port, pack-side alcohol, decisions
- [largecontent](#largecontent): karma, mod additions, sticky notes
- [ports](#ports): Exposure, feature porting, Caverns and Chasms, Autumnity, baby mobs, Veggies Delight, Vintage Delight, Fungi Delight, Cultural Delights (pinned)
- [creativedelight](#creativedelight): framework, members, QoL moves, swappers
- [creativebrewing](#creativebrewing): potion brewing, alcohol, effect text and effect changes, planned as their own mod
- [textures](#textures): food sprites, block / mob / gear art, merge-family art, texture tooling
- [dumps](#dumps): changes blocked on a fresh dump, with their queue entries
- [merges](#merges): item merges and splits (dishes, pancakes, teas, fruit, crates and bags, cardboard, rope, monkeys)
- [pack](#pack): release, server, docs
- [tooling](#tooling): KubeJS helpers and conventions, dictionaries, patcherMod cleanup, generators and tests
- [archive](#archive): every **Complete** row, by family

ST-228 to ST-231, SQ-DW-01 to 03. Still open: the sunk log (investigated 2026-10-25) is most likely Geophilic's **bush**, not its stump: every bush (`bushes/mc/*`) has trunk height 0 and `dirt_provider` = its log, so the grass block under it becomes a log and the leaves sit at ground level; acacia's is a flat 3x3 mat at ~4 per savanna chunk (others ~1.2). Fix: trunk height 1 + dirt provider dirt (vanilla jungle bush shape). Confirm in game first: leaves around the sunk log = bush. A single stump + fallen log feature (1.21.5 `fallen_tree` shape) would need patcherMod code; the metas cover the grouping | bush fix for acacia only or all woods | [structure_tuning.md](../structure_tuning.md) 3.16, 3.25; scratchpad 2026-10-22 |

Cause: vanilla's biome lookup keeps the thread's last result on a tie, and Terralith's `cave/deep_caves` ties with `deep_dark` (depth 1.05-1.2, erosion below -0.375), so the census thread and the worldgen thread can see different biomes. Fixed 2026-10-27 (`worldgen/TiedBiomes`): the biome stage and the biome-factor roll only say no when every tied biome fails. Harness PASS (9 checks, 20000 random points); not installed. Live: SQ-TIE-01 | none | [locate.md](../locate.md) 3.6 |

| 1 | Block dyeing and soaping audit | Planned | patcherMod / KubeJS / docs | Go through every block in game and decide which may be dyed with a dye and which washed with soap in world, and which are disabled. In-world dye is Supplementaries `[tweaks.dye_blocks]` over every Moonlight colour set (any block id with all 16 `<color>_` / `_<color>` variants); since 2026-10-20 blocks with a block entity are skipped and plain blocks go in `#creativeengineering:no_world_dye` (`SupplementariesDyeBlocksMixin`). Soap (`SoapWashableHelper`) clears colour sets back to the base block (copies block-entity data; config `clean_blacklist`, beds skipped), unwaxes / unoxidises, and washes `IWashable` blocks (Supplementaries text holders, bamboo spikes). Sophisticated Backpacks has neither (checked 2026-10-20). To check: a container that drops its contents when removed and is soapable could duplicate; whether shulker boxes, banners, presents should dye in world again (needs block-entity carry-over); other mods with their own in-world dye or wash (vanilla cauldron, signs, Quark, Create, Botania) AUDIT WRITTEN 2026-10-27 ([dyeing.md](../dyeing.md) section 2): mechanisms D1 to D6 / S1 to S4 / W1 to W3, both / soap-only / dye-only / neither tables, wrong results (tag-joined Fantasy Furniture beds and candles, soul candles, coffee candle cakes probably change variant when dyed; valve handle and toolbox wash to white; rusted IW iron not washed), unclean-state soap candidates (mossy, overgrown, rusty, dirty, sandy, ashen), proposals P1 to P6, checks DY-01 to DY-06. OWNER DECISIONS 2026-10-27 written up in [dyeing.md](../dyeing.md) section 3 (disables, banner limit, Botania excluded, dye carry-over, soap targets and families, unclean soap, tool interactions; DY-07 to DY-13), build not started | open questions in dyeing.md 3.8;  allow list per block family; a no_world_soap tag if soap needs one | this file; mixin javadoc |

# alcohol

Alcohol is the two ports plus the pack around them, grouped here from `largecontent` and the other families on
2026-10-19: `brewinport` and `vineryport` (the two jars) and `pack` (pack-side alcohol rows). The open calls are in the
decisions table under the main one. Rows that stay elsewhere because they cover more than alcohol: `pack/release #5`
(`/dumpall` refresh with the ports), `ui/names #2` (`vinery:trippy` lang), `jei/jei-coverage #2` (Vinery world
interactions). Both port jars (`-02`) are live since 1.0.8. First in-game results 2026-10-20 (local `-03`, [alcohol.md](../alcohol.md) 10.18:
bottle sizes, box looks, Stained Glass, wine box and flying fixes); AL-01 to AL-72 and VB-01 to VB-66 are still the first
job before any balance change.

Karma example actions, moved verbatim from the scratchpad:
- Good: planting saplings (low limit); bonemeal to compost (low); trading with villagers (low); helping in raids
  (cooldown, high); killing pillagers and witches (cooldown, low); killing the Ender Dragon (exponential decrease,
  negative offset after x kills?); breeding animals (linear decrease to 0, low); taming pets (linear decrease to low,
  low); playing with pets (high karma, high limit); setting pet spawn points (linear decrease to low, cooldown, high);
  curing zombie villagers (slow linear decrease to low, high); tilling, planting, harvesting, planting flowers (high
  limit, slow linear increase, 5 min cooldown); food pantry server feature?; writing books, signing albums (linear
  decrease to low, high boost, high limit); taking photos (low boost, low limit); Via Romana path creation (linear
  increase to high, high limit).
- Bad: breaking claimed beds (small for villager-owned, large for player-owned, linear increase on repeats); being
  mean (no cooldown, high limit: talking rude to MCA villagers, punching villagers or baby animals, killing villagers,
  more for baby villagers and golems); pet respawning (high limit, exponential, no cooldown); killing pacifist or
  protected mobs (exponential, high limit, 1 min cooldown: bats, dolphins, axolotls, pandas, polar bears, elephants,
  whales, Alex's Mobs with no drops: review); killing animals (slow linear from low, 10 s cooldown, buffer of no
  effect); igniting TNT (constant low, no cooldown); breaking a village bell (exponential, no cooldown, high);
  breaking wild waystones (small linear if never activated, large exponential if another player activated it, and a
  chat announcement naming the destroyer); igniting a Nether portal (failing: one large decrease with a low limit,
  chat announcement the first time per player; succeeding: slow linear decrease, low limit, first-time announcement);
  attacking neutral mobs (slow linear, high cap: zombie pigmen, passive spiders, hitting or looking at endermen);
  stealing honey without a campfire (low exponential, low limit); refuelling a boat (slow linear, low limit); falling
  into the void (exponential, 5 min cooldown, high limit); looting another player's corpse (quick exponential, high
  limit).

# creativedelight

The Creative Delight jar (`development/creativeDelight`) and everything that moves into it. Opened 2026-10-25 from
[creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md); rows were taken from `ports/creativedelightport` and `food/types-and-tuning #14`.
Subfamilies are A to Z, which is also the work order: **framework** (loader, build, tests, dict, config, harness) first, then
**members** (each Delight mod's rows and Java moving in), **qol** (QoL-1 to QoL-6 leaving patcherMod and the pack folder),
**swappers** (later). A member row needs framework #2 (install), #4 (dict) and #6 (move-diff harness) first; a QoL row needs framework #5 (config key) and, for
Java, #8 (compartments). Owner rule: no ongoing backport or merge features beyond what these rows say.

## framework

Never while the game runs | after row 1 passes | [creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md) 19.5 (was ports/creativedelightport #2) [brief](../briefs/creativedelight_framework.md) |

# creativebrewing

A new own mod, Creative Brewing, planned to hold what Creative Delight does not take (owner, 2026-10-25: "mark all potion brewing, alcohol, vinery, and effect descriptions, changes, shuffles to be moved to a new mod, Creative Brewing"). Nothing moved yet.

## move

| # | Change | Status | Work | Details | Decisions | Writeup |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Move potion brewing, alcohol, Vinery and effect content into Creative Brewing | Planned | KubeJS / new jar | Marked 2026-10-25 during the CD food move (creative_delight.md 29.3). Left in the pack for it: `startup_scripts/cooking/potions.js`, `startup_scripts/cooking/alcohol_drinks.js`, `server_scripts/cooking/alcohol.js`, the effect swaps and Farmer's Respite cake-slice effects in `startup_scripts/cooking/food_values.js`, the Brewin' and Chewin' food rows in `server_scripts/cooking/cooking.js` (pierogi, pizza, ham and cheese sandwich, quiches, horror lasagna, creamy onion soup, cheesy pasta, vegetable omelet), the Brewin' tags in `server_scripts/tags/cookingtags.js`, the effect text (`other/jeed_providers.js`, JEED and tooltip effect lines), and the Brewin' and Vinery ports. Needs: the mod project, its own script trees like CD's, and a call on whether alcohol-side food rows (Brewin' dishes) go there or to CD | open: the mod's scope edge with CD (Brewin' dishes, ciders) | [creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md) 29.3 |
